The token is fixture-authored and deliberate: `run_p2_suite_v3.py:1432` sets it as the env-var **name** (value `"1"`) for A13's env sub-run, and the production code echoes that same name from a compile-time constant (`regbase/LatePipeline.cpp:350` `getenv(<NAME>)`, `:352` prints the name, `:353` the value), so A13's coupling to the production constant is real and its PASS is earned. **Two harms avoided:** quoting the masked form would put into the control record a string that no artefact contains, so no third party could ever reproduce the locator (D081's failure mode, different mechanism); quoting the on-disk form would transcribe a credential-shaped literal into a control document, against the standing no-leak rule. **Class:** the matcher-literal family (D042) generalised -- what a tool *shows* is not automatically what a file *contains*. | **Recorded (Lead, P2-B full-run audit, 2026-10-14)** | Measured in-session: `grep -rIl '{{SECRET_'` over `/home/s00653124/workspace/hivm-poc-evidence/p2-20261013-late-pipeline/` = 0 files; `b"{{" in <suite-summary.json bytes>` = False; regex locator `\[ERROR\] Late mode: (\S+) is set \(value` over the raw bytes yields a 27-byte token, sha256 `b634c103...`, charset `[A-Z0-9_]`; the two full-run rows whose detail quotes it are A13's env sub-run. `docs/status.md` 追加 48 §一 |

| D087 | **The P2-C compiled-in expected-template SHA is DEFINED as "the C310 template this compiler build was built against, and which the staged bundle ships" — it is neither the D031/P1 pin nor a correctness proof, and the gate it drives must be labelled for what it is: a substitution / post-build-drift detector.** Raised and ruled by the Lead 2026-10-14 while reviewing the implementor's P2-C plan (msg `64ddb492`). **The forcing measurement:** the fresh P2-C template is NOT byte-equal to the P1 pin — O2 `5e7ccae2…`(31,206,796 B) → `3807eb3d…`(31,206,992 B) = **+196 B**; O0 `0a34be87…`(27,695,180 B) → `b8c68dcb…`(27,695,840 B) = **+660 B**; `.linked.bc` `1b356045…` → `cbd56bd9…` = **+644 B**; first differing byte is **37** in O2 and O0, and the deltas are **unequal** (O2 additionally has a ccec post-link edge), so a uniform substitution is ruled out. **The mechanism, disclosed rather than hidden:** because the constant is derived from the build it guards, the N2 arm asserts "nothing swapped the template after this compiler was built" — it does **not** assert the template's content is correct. The correctness burden is therefore carried explicitly by three other artefacts, which must be named beside the constant so no reader inflates the gate: the S2 recorded identities (two rows, two labels, never one — D081), B5/B6 (the 4th i32 store operand / cache4 **inside** the template), and the swap-the-template differential (the leg that proves the linker reads *that* file). **Consequential branch:** if the template build proves **non-reproducible**, the constant is a property of **one build**, not of the tree, so a rebuild of identical source invalidates it and it must be regenerated — P3 inherits that. Decided by a required discriminator (same inputs to two different `-o` paths; `strings`/`llvm-bcanalyzer` diff; and whether `DMAUtils.h`'s content or only its mtime changed). **Also ruled in the same review:** `runLateBitcodeAndA5` is declared in the **existing** `regbase/PassPipeline.h` (not a new header, not a file-local duplicate declaration, where a signature drift can hide), making the P2-C cut a **five-file** change — safe only because D079 state (b) pinned P2-B's cut by commit, not by worktree state; the commit message must state the five-file scope. §0's split rows are the Lead's to place; the implementor must **not** edit `p2-binary-abi.md`. | **RULED (Lead 2026-10-14)** — subject to the implementor's discriminator (which decides only the provenance, not the ruling) and to **Challenger confirmation at §8 step 8**. | `docs/status.md` 追加 51 (the measured deltas) and 追加 53 (this review); `docs/p2-binary-abi.md` §0's pre-declared split rule ("the two are different claims about possibly the same bytes, and this record must never collapse them"); msg `4a97dc4b`/`b6ea79a3`/`64ddb492`. §0's **6 NOT_RUN** identity rows stay NOT_RUN — all depend on a real Late compile, and none was filled by inference (goal §7). |
| D088 | **P2-C's S1(a) left the D031 bitcode pin DIRTY-AND-ARMED: `build/lib/meta_op.aiv.c310.{O2,O0}.bc` is a declared ninja output whose 54+54 per-file inputs are now newer than it, so any explicit consumer regenerates the pin in place — bounded, because the default target does not reach it.** Raised and measured by the Lead 2026-10-14 while reviewing the implementor's S1(a). **The measurement.** `build/build.ninja:216819` declares `build lib/meta_op.aiv.c310.O2.bc: CUSTOM_COMMAND lib/meta_op.aiv.c310.O2.bc.linked.bc` (a ccec edge over the llvm-link intermediate), so the pin IS a ninja output, not a static artifact. S1(a) rebuilt the 108 per-file template bitcodes at 08:39 today; **54 O2 and 54 O0 per-file inputs now postdate the pin** (pin mtimes 2026-10-13 13:46), and `…O2.bc.linked.bc` is likewise still at 13:46, so **both the pin and its intermediate are dirty**. **The bound, which corrects the first, too-strong reading of this hazard.** `build.ninja:288795` is `default all`, and the token `install-bishengir-publish-products` is **absent** from the `build all:` input list (checked textually) — so a **plain `ninja` does not reach the template targets** and cannot rewrite the pin. S1(a) survived only because it targeted the per-file outputs rather than their consumers. **Rule derived (binds P2-C, P2-D and P3):** no step may build any consumer of the C310 template — `install-bishengir-publish-products`, any `bishengir_link_*` phony, or a dependent — because doing so overwrites the D031 pin's on-disk bytes; and the pin's identity must be **read from a staged bundle with `base_dir` stated**, never from `build/lib`, whose copy is now the least trustworthy of the five. **The identity survives regardless (measured):** `5e7ccae2…` / `0a34be87…` are held byte-identical in four independent bundles — `impl/stage-p2a/lib/`, `impl/stage-p2a-r1/lib/`, `impl/stage-p2b/lib/`, `impl/stage-p2b-r1/lib/` — so the frozen binding has a repoint target even if `build/lib` is overwritten (the D048/D062 remedy pattern). | **Recorded (Lead-measured 2026-10-14).** Completes the class D048/D062 named ("every absolute path in the frozen bindings that lies under the build tree is a baseline the P2 build deterministically pre-empts") for the bitcode pin, and adds the first measurement of the *dirty* condition itself. | D090 | **A Late-mode positive run must execute a staged bundle whose sibling `lib/` holds that bundle's own templates; the build tree is not a valid positive-test environment, because the identity gate fails closed on the build tree's copy by design.** The template directory is `../lib` derived from the executable's **real path** (`getLibDirFromExecutable`: real_path, strip the filename, append `../lib`), and the gate compares the template it resolves there against the value compiled into that binary. Measured on the P2-C binary: `build/bin/bishengir-compile` against `build/lib/` gives rc=1, `a5-compile=0`, `kernel.o` ABSENT, `reason: sha256-mismatch` -- observed `5e7ccae2…` (the P1 pin) against the P2-C build's own expected value. A positive row aimed at `build/bin` therefore **FAILS**, for the correct reason but not the assertion its author meant: a **false-FAIL waiting to be written**, the mirror of a false-PASS, and it would read as a broken freeze rather than as a designed refusal. **Corollary:** a symlinked `bin/` entry silently re-points `../lib` at the build tree, because the derivation follows the real path -- so a bundle cannot be published by symlinking its way into the build tree. **Discipline:** every Late positive row states its executable path and the `lib/` it must resolve, and reads any baseline template identity from a staged bundle with `base_dir` stated (D088). | **Recorded (Lead, from the implementor's four-arm smoke, 2026-10-14; pending independent confirmation by the verifier's frozen rows)** | `impl/scratch/p2c-smoke/smoke-late-run.log` arms A-D; `impl/scratch/p2c-smoke/late-20261014T005231Z/{A,B,C,D}/`; `impl/stage-p2c-r1/SHA256SUMS.txt`; `impl/scratch/p2c-build/s4-build-2.log` (4 edges, pin untouched); `build/lib` pin hashes `5e7ccae2…`/`0a34be87…` mtime 10-13 13:46 re-measured by Lead; `docs/status.md` 追加 56; msgs `c4be29d5`, `08c95ef0`. Family D087/D088. |
| D091 | **The D031/P1 template pin was compiled from a pre-commit working-tree state that is no longer on disk, so it has NO rebuild path and must never be "verified" by rebuilding it.** Measured: the pin's differing debug info is attributed to one header, `RegBase/DMAUtils.h`, and an anchor test over that header's candidate revisions scores the pin 12/34 against `80e29e6f2` (the only commit touching the header since 2026-09-17), 4/34 against its parent `b345e38e4`, 22/34 against `be6b9490e`, and 12/34 against the current worktree -- **no committed revision reproduces it** -- while the P2 template scores **34/34** against the worktree, i.e. the positive control shows the method can reach a perfect score on a state a template really was built from. The pin's object-build window is 2026-10-13 13:37:55-13:46:02; the header's worktree mtime is 14:42:53, **after** that window and **before** the 19:47 commit, so the pin came from an intermediate uncommitted state that a later worktree edit overwrote. **Corollary 1:** the pin's identity can only be **preserved as bytes** (D031, D088); a rebuild cannot "confirm" it -- any procedure that regenerates it does not verify the pin, it silently replaces it. **Corollary 2 (closes an earlier wrong attribution):** the pin **already** carries `%l2_cache_mode` in the same 39 define-lines and the same 1677 `llvm.hivm.store.vfsimt.info` calls as the P2 template (diff 0), so the +196 B O2 delta is **not** the cache-mode change (the earlier implementor note saying so is withdrawn); the two artefacts are the same code and differ only in debug line/column/scope-line tables of that one header. **Scope limit (disclosed, not smoothed):** the content comparison is over **re-emitted** IR (the producer's own clang 15 frontend; LLVM 19.1.7 refuses this bitcode and no LLVM-15 `llvm-dis`/`llvm-bcanalyzer` is installed), so a raw difference the re-emission normalises away on both sides would be invisible. | **Recorded (Lead, independently reproduced 2026-10-14)** | `impl/scratch/p2c-template-probe/TEMPLATE-DIFFERENTIAL-PROBE.txt` sections 1-7; Lead's own reproductions: 1,325,498 non-metadata lines per side with a 0-line diff; 803,035 metadata-definition lines per side with 57,584 changed lines, of which 508 DISubprogram lines whose distinct `file:` reference resolves to exactly one `DIFile` (`DMAUtils.h`); 39 define-lines carrying `%l2_cache_mode` per side; 1677 store-descriptor calls per side with a 0-line diff; `instr.diff` and `meta-norm2.diff` both 0 bytes; plus `git log`/`git show` on the header (2377 to 2428 lines, `l2_cache_mode` 0 to 16 occurrences). `docs/status.md` 追加 58. Family D031/D087/D088. |
| D092 | **A masking layer rewrites text in BOTH directions — tool output *and the command text I send to the tools* — so an identity can be altered in flight either way; such a rendering is not width-preserving, not invertible, not reproducible, and not detectable by re-running the same command, so an identity is sealed only after a grouped read-back against an independently computed value.** Measured 2026-10-14 on the P2-C work: (i) *output direction* — a `sha256sum` render of one unrelated `lib` member came back with a placeholder **inside** the 64-hex digest (family `PHONE_…`, distinct from D082's `SECRET_…`); the rendering's surviving head (20 hex chars) and tail (33 hex chars) are true substrings of the digest, so 11 hex chars were replaced by an 18-char placeholder — deleting it yields 53 chars, not 64, so the value can only be **re-measured**, never "repaired"; (ii) *input direction* — in one python invocation the same placeholder typed **contiguously** measured `len == 11` while the identical string **assembled from pieces** measured `18` and compared unequal, i.e. the literal did not reach the interpreter intact; (iii) *not reproducible* — 8 fresh direct readings (5 single-file `sha256sum`, 3 whole-directory globs) all rendered unmasked and all equalled the in-process digest, so the file is intact and "run it again" is not a detector. **Forced action, executed here:** every identity sealed in this window — the P2-C artifact, the v3 source manifest and its generator, the `base..HEAD` diff, the five committed file hashes, this record's own hash, and the six template-split values — was read back through a **grouped** rendering and compared against a fresh measurement: **15/15 full-value citations reproduce**, the single non-hit being a deliberate 8-hex **prefix** citation in `docs/status.md`, not a masking artifact. Corollary, recorded because the breach was mine: I first transcribed the placeholder *into* `docs/status.md` labelled as an undamaged 64-hex value — a D082 breach whose concrete harm is a **false tamper alarm** for a later reader — and corrected it by one **disclosed** in-place rewrite, with the original text quoted (placeholder removed) in 追加 60 §一. | Recorded (Lead, measured 2026-10-14; the in-place rewrite is flagged for Challenger review at goal §8 step 8) | `docs/status.md` 追加 59 §六(2) (corrected in place, disclosed) and 追加 60 §§一–四; `docs/p2-binary-abi.md` §0, whose six values were the control set |
| D093 | **An oracle value must not live in a file that the artefact's own documentation declares free to amend: where the value's record sits outside the bundle's checksum coverage, the freeze must anchor that record from OUTSIDE by registering the recording file's sha256 + byte count, and acceptance must re-measure it — a mismatch is FAIL and "it is only prose, it may be amended" is not an excuse.** The P2-C staged bundle's own `SHA256SUMS.README.txt` states of `TEMPLATE-IDENTITY.txt` (the file carrying the §6.2 expected template value) that the two prose files "are NOT covered by SHA256SUMS.txt and are free to be amended"; and indeed `sha256sum -c SHA256SUMS.txt` returns 4/4 OK no matter how that file is rewritten, because the covered set is `bin/` + `lib/` only. So the value's *reproducibility* (proven: a grouped D092 read-back gives record == tool == independent hashlib, 6 identities × 8 readings) is not the missing property — the missing property is immutability of the recording FILE, which only a manifest outside the bundle can supply. Today's anchors: `TEMPLATE-IDENTITY.txt` `9def87708f2491c91c2fce031cbecd47af484bae311cabb12f640b3eb47ebd47` / 5716 B, `SHA256SUMS.README.txt` `12cdb74b17c648c5ed0678605742b32218464ac56f9a9d7eb70895d300f6f5f6` / 4924 B. | Lead ruling **R3**, issued 2026-10-14 **before** the P2-C freeze and sent to the verifier as a freeze-manifest requirement; enforceable by re-measurement at P2-C acceptance | `docs/status.md` 追加 61 §四–§五; `impl/stage-p2c-r1/SHA256SUMS.README.txt` (4924 B `12cdb74b…`) and `TEMPLATE-IDENTITY.txt` (5716 B `9def8770…`); read-back log `impl/scratch/p2c-build/s2-identity-readback.log` (2370 B `c9c36436…`), independently re-run by the Lead |

| D095 | **A documented collision is not a handled collision: when a marker literal already recorded in this register as SHARED finally gets its first executable consumer, the collision must be resolved at the KEY, not merely noted in the register — otherwise the consumer's first correct verdict is a false FAIL.** Measured 2026-10-14 on the P2-C freeze's first consumer. D075 recorded that the a5-once marker payload is carried by two instrumentation sites (`regbase/BiShengIRCompileMain.cpp` `[P2-C-GATE] a5-compile entry=runHIVMCCompileA5 route=late` and `hivmc/A5/HIVMCMainA5.cpp` `[P2-BOUNDARY] a5-compile entry=runHIVMCCompileA5`) and that no executable consumer existed. The consumer now exists: `verify/p2c-freeze/a5_marker_count.py` keys on `A5_KEY = "a5-compile entry=runHIVMCCompileA5"` (`:45`) — the **bare substring** — while §6 of the same freeze freezes `a5 count == 1 -> PASS`, `>= 2 -> FAIL`. Third-party read-only census of the subject binary (`impl/scratch/p2c-build/p2c-binary-marker-presence.log`, 09:45:06): in the P2-C staged binary (`impl/stage-p2c-r1/bin/bishengir-compile`, `812c5cb1d29cb3b8879593ea77d08ebdae274cc943d41523c7c4bab739cbf455`, 205,469,200 B) the bare token occurs **2×** — @16115604 inside the GATE line, @20099878 inside the BOUNDARY line — against **1×** in the P2-B control (`ea217b8d…`). So the freeze's own rule would FAIL the first correct Late run. **Ruled: the oracle keys on the prefix-anchored decomposition** (`[P2-BOUNDARY] a5-compile entry=runHIVMCCompileA5` count == 1 **AND** `[P2-C-GATE] … route=late` count == 1), which is what `docs/p2-architecture.md:1370-1377` already defines and `docs/p2-pass-trace.md` A4 already anchors; a consumer fix is acceptable but moves the consumer's sha, which §6 and §9 must then record. **Two corollaries.** (i) The census that produced this evidence is **not** the A4 row: its 19 `want=/found=` lines are string-PRESENCE checks, not a pass count, and must never be read as A4 PASS (D083 family). (ii) When an existing row's subject binary is replaced, every marker set derived from the OLD binary must be re-measured — the freeze's §6 set is still the `ea217b8d`-era six lines. | Lead ruling **R-1**, issued 2026-10-14 **before** the freeze was accepted; binding on the P2-C freeze and on any later consumer of a P2-BOUNDARY-family marker | `docs/status.md` 追加 69 §三(R-1) and 追加 71 §§二–三; `verify/p2c-freeze/P2C-TEST-FREEZE.txt` §6 (`676223cf45cc08047f7e163790ecb33fb080e672da2f8fd9b957728c1cdada5f`, 43,271 B) and `a5_marker_count.py` (`9df1d6437abc09f7d074265310f908cd59959d154f31da5267967d05b6136d7d`, 8,473 B); `impl/scratch/p2c-build/p2c-binary-marker-presence.{log,p2b-control.log}`. | D097 | **A count of the literals an artefact EMBEDS is not a count of the lines that artefact EMITS: an expected runtime count must be sourced from a real run's stream, and the embedding census is admissible only as the diagnosis that the literals are compiled in at all.** Measured 2026-10-14 on the P2-C bundle binary `812c5cb1d29cb3b8879593ea77d08ebdae274cc943d41523c7c4bab739cbf455`: the read-only census reports `[P2-BOUNDARY] family found=6` and `[P2-C-GATE] family found=7` **distinct embedded marker strings**, while the run of record (`impl/scratch/p2c-smoke/late-20261014T005329Z/A/stderr`, 1816 B / 13 lines / rc=0) emits **2 BOUNDARY + 9 C-GATE** marker lines — 7 literals emit 9 lines because the `attach` literal fires once per C310 slot and the two `a5-result` literals sit on opposite branches of which one fires — and the three fail-closed arms B/C/D each emit **1 BOUNDARY + 3 C-GATE** lines carrying **zero** bare a5 tokens (rc=1 each). So an expected line count written from the census would be wrong on **every** arm, and wrong in **opposite directions at once** (one family over-counted 7→9, the other under-counted 6→2). The consumer `a5_marker_count.py` is safe only because it counts `stream.count(m)` per embedded marker, i.e. it measures the stream, and asserts no fixed number. Also measured: the archived ordinary control `verify/logs/suite-v3/20261013T232010Z-2744262/D4.ordinary.stderr` (582 B / 8 lines) emits the bare token **once**, on a `[P2-BOUNDARY]` line, so "the bare token appears twice" is a property of the Late route under **this** binary, not of the token. Distinct from D083 (which mis-counted the population of *rows*); same family — **a count must name its population, and here the population is the run's stream, not the binary's string table.** Supersedes nothing; family D042/D083/D095. |

R-10's erratum channel is NOT sufficient here.** | **R-10 ("a discovery after the seal is an erratum in REVISION.txt, never an in-place edit to the body") protects the DIGEST, which is why r3 must not be rewritten; it does not license leaving false instruction in the body.** Revisions (1), (3), (4), (5) change text a future executor reads AS the criterion, and revision (3) changes the sealed consumer's own output string, which invalidates the census's declared `consumer-sha256` and therefore forces the 28-row table to be regenerated — so the corrected census must live in the new body, not beside it. Not an in-place edit: r3 keeps its identity and stops being operative, exactly as r1→r2→r3. **Approved matters, recorded so the revision does not disturb them:** entry and pass cut APPROVE BY SOURCE at HEAD `4495ab29b350f9285337de66a6a19f0e8bc68510` (formal execution NOT_RUN); R-1's observation/requirement split APPROVE; D097's populations independently reproduced (6/7 embedded, 2/9 reference, 1/3 per fail-closed arm); E-G omission guards APPROVE (both omitted, each omitted, and `nan` all return 3); the 28 table triples match script, seal and read-only replay with zero mismatches; all five principal seals unchanged across the review; 30 NOT_RUN lines; no compiler row credited as PASS. |

Recovery was a byte-exact restore from the Lead's own earlier `cp -p` snapshots (`/tmp/r4ex/…`, `/tmp/lead-a5ex-exp2/…`, both re-hashed to the registered `6059babe…`) — **same class as the sidecar's recorded pre-E-G consumer recovery, not a regeneration**; the only irreversible delta is ctime, which no registration cites. **The class lesson is not about `py_compile`:** the harness-isolation memory already said "a harness is not isolated because it is read-only toward the *sealed* artifact" and this is that sentence firing on the *reviewer*. **Correct practice** (any one): `python3 -B -c "compile(open(p).read(), p, 'exec')"`, `ast.parse(open(p).read())`, `compile()` to a temp cfile, or `cp` to an isolated workdir first — the criterion is that the check produce **zero bytes**, never "I believed it was read-only". **A shared tree admits no undeclared write-capable action:** the fix for `UNIDENTIFIED` is not better forensics but **declaring the write**, and the incidental fact that recovery was possible at all rested on snapshots taken for other reasons — luck, not design. |

| D101 | **The §6.2 item-5 cache4 claim has two halves and only the first is measured; the second is an INFERENCE and must be reported as one.** (a) The compiler EMITS the value 4 as the store libcall's 4th `i32` operand — measured in the run of record (`C2.stderr`: `llvm.call @store_ubuf_to_gm_1d_float(..., %3, %1)` with `%1 = llvm.mlir.constant(4 : i32)`; at the HIVM level, `hivm.hir.store … {l2_cache_mode = 4 : i32}` → `call @store_ubuf_to_gm_1d_float(%cast_5, %cast_6, %c0_i32, %c4_i32)`). (b) The TEMPLATE consumes a value in that position as a cache mode — measured **from the probe's own re-emitted IR** (`impl/scratch/p2c-template-probe/dis15/p2.o2.ll:743742`): the entry `@_mlir_ciface_store_ubuf_to_gm_1d_float`'s 4th parameter is named `%l2_cache_mode` and is `zext`-ed (`:743860`) then `shl`-ed by 60 (`:743861`), OR-ed into the descriptor word (`:743862-863`) and passed to `@llvm.hivm.MOV.UB.TO.OUT.ALIGN.V2.DV` (`:743866`); 104 such sites, identical in `p1.o2.ll`. **The third thing — the LINKED artifact's own machine code — is NOT observed:** in the run of record the store is still a declared `llvm.func private` (the body arrives from the template at link time), the template body is absent from the dumped module (0 `MOV.UB.TO.OUT`, 0 `l2_cache_mode` in the final region), and the emitted ELF is machine `0x1029` (AIV), which no host disassembler decodes (the build tree's `llvm-objdump` dies on `GLIBCXX_3.4.29/30`; host LLVM 15 cannot read the producer's 15.0.5 bitcode). So "the linked kernel consumes the 4 the emitter wrote" is an inference from (a)+(b) plus a successful link. | **RULED — (a) and (b) MEASURED; the linked-artifact half NOT ESTABLISHED, recorded as an inference.** This is R1's residue. | **Evidence:** `C2.stderr` (run of record); `impl/scratch/p2c-template-probe/dis15/{p1,p2}.o2.ll`; `lead/r9-review/STEP9-EVIDENCE-MAP.txt` (the §6.2 item-5 open question) and `lead/r9-review/STEP8-VERDICT-INTERIM.txt` (V-7 addendum). **The one unspent positive evidence compile (obligation C8) is NOT spent on this** — R1's own instruction is to inspect existing disassembly first and to authorize a differential separately. |

Rows 追加 232 and 追加 233 had themselves been sealed carrying a literal `__TS__` (their append commands wrote the placeholder but performed no substitution), so the global sed rewrote sealed bytes: the sealed prefix's sha256 moved from `1f76c2e61a87b809ad25824733e55ca0a3eb2184d94feb3e816b342f073b141b` to `d535717350661e45298c552f3ba5c6b71b27573fff4cf33c8019680ff8b4d88f`. **The prefix check inside that same command caught it** (`head -c 1437553 | sha256sum` printed `d5357173…` ≠ the expected `1f76c2e6…`) — precisely the failure mode the prefix check exists to catch, and it fired on the first opportunity. **Restoration:** reversing the 2 substitutions inside the old region `[0,1437575)` and leaving the new block untouched reproduced `head -c 1437553` = `1f76c2e6…`, **byte-exactly** (measured at 1,447,933 B). A SECOND, independent error in the same action: the substituted value `2026-10-14T20:41Z` was itself WRONG for 232/233 (those rows were written at 20:18Z / 20:24Z), so even a "successful" global substitution would have written a wrong value — one action, two defects. **(b) MEASURED — the four artifacts, each with the placeholder site, its SEALED identity, and its actual write time (UTC, from the session transcript's own tool_use timestamps):** `docs/status.md` 追加 232, line 12551, 1,434,874 B / `3ec11eb4ff9ad43455629b86dd14d5fd162c75ec933a4a99027999f19e38193b`, written `2026-10-14T20:18:12Z`; `docs/status.md` 追加 235 names the defect table and 追加 236 gives 235's own time slot, which a bounded-substitution uniqueness assertion refused — correctly — because the marker occurred twice in its payload). **The general rule, stated so it is not re-learned:** a template placeholder must be substituted BEFORE sealing; a placeholder that survives sealing makes the label present and the value absent, and no reader can tell from the bytes whether that means "not yet measured" or "forgot to substitute". **Substitution must be BOUNDED and its literal must be UNIQUE in the target region** — an unbounded or non-unique substitution either rewrites sealed bytes (as here) or rewrites the wrong occurrence. **No conclusion moves:** the R6 adjudication (追加 234) is unchanged — A13 / A15 / R1 remain unclosed test-side defects requiring a v5 revision, no aggregation into a P2 PASS, P3 gate stays CLOSED. | **Evidence:** the transcript's own tool_use/tool_result pair at `2026-10-14T20:41:01.767Z` (the failing prefix check, `d5357173…`); `docs/status.md` 追加 234/235/236. | D117 | **A13's `plain` sub-run is an UNSATISFIABLE criterion at P2-C — a THIRD and DIFFERENT defect mode from D044's `env` mode, proven pre-run from the bound binary; A15 and R1 are stale-expectation defects of the same family. Dispatched as a v5 runner revision.** **MEASURED (Lead, 2026-10-15, from sources and the bound binary — not from a failed run):** `t_a13`'s `plain` branch requires `label in ("STUB-BARE","STUB-TERMINAL") and rc != 0`, i.e. it requires the P2-B universal stub to be REACHABLE. The P2-C artifact of record `impl/stage-p2c-r1/bin/bishengir-compile` (sha256 `812c5cb1d29cb3b8879593ea77d08ebdae274cc943d41523c7c4bab739cbf455`, 205,469,200 B) contains the stub's distinctive phrase `not implemented in this build` **0** times and the real terminal phrase `This route has no fallback` **2** times; the P2-B predecessor `stage-p2b-r1` contains the stub phrase **1** time. `stub_terminal()` therefore returns False on every stream the P2-C binary can emit, so the branch is unsatisfiable for EVERY input and A13 is FAIL by construction. The row's own v4 comment already states the correct rule ("At a later stage the stub is replaced by the real tail, so classify by stage expectation rather than hard-failing") while the code hard-fails — the comment and the code disagree, and the code is what runs. Run evidence consistent with this (not the proof): `A13.plain` rc=0, stderr 1,816 B ending `[P2-C-GATE] a5-result rc=success`, `plain.o` sha256 `2c4247c3…` — the SAME artifact as `A1/kernel.o`; v3 and v4 `A13.plain.stderr` are byte-identical (sha256 `7f43c384…`). **DISTINCT FROM D044:** D044's defect was the `env` sub-run's INVOCATION never creating the condition it asserted (no matcher repair possible); this one is a MATCHER whose expectation was superseded by the P2-C artifact, and it is provable BEFORE any run, from the bound binary's own strings. Both are false greens of the same family but neither subsumes the other: fixing `env`'s provisioning does not make `plain` satisfiable, and re-scoping `plain` does not repair `env`. **A15/R1 (same family, stale expectation):** A15's flag loop invokes the compiler WITHOUT `--mlir-print-ir-after-all` yet its rc==0 branch asserts `_store_cache_state(out, err)` form/val==4 — an assertion whose input the invocation never produces; R1's own code returns FAIL on rc=0 with the note that it "asserts the P2-B fail-closed route, never a successful Late compile", and under P2-C plain Late SUCCEEDS, so its premise is gone. `t_a15` and `t_r1` have NO hunk in the v3→v4 diff (byte-identical bodies), so v4 did not touch them. **Challenger corroboration (independent, from the sealed verdict):** `lead/r9-review/STEP8-VERDICT-FINAL.txt` (sha256 `25c87857…`, 20,899 B) `:105` names all three in one defect table — A13 "Its plain-Late control still requires the superseded P2-B stub", A15 "Commands omit the IR-dump option required by their cache-operand assertion", R1 "It requires P2-B's superseded fail-closed positive behavior". | **DISPATCHED to the verifier as a v5 runner revision (msg `84a3a1f3-4a47-430c-b07b-c486bd70c1d1`), NOT yet delivered; P2-C and P2-D stay REVISE and the P3 gate stays CLOSED.** The three are test-side, not compiler defects. Required of v5: preserve v4 bytes `4ce88ad732dc50c2cb215a609d2039f025bf7d4faaa6ab5935cf1aed66879be0`; keep the v4 run dir read-only; write `suite-v5/<RUN_ID>` with an independent workdir; leave `verify/work/run` (40 tags, A1 `kernel.o` `2c4247c3…` / 4,448 B) and the historical 37/6/3 unchanged; give BOTH directions of no-weakening control per row (an A13 control that can pass on a stub-terminal stream is not a control); declare an explicit NOT_RUN list; and aggregate nothing into a P2 PASS. | Evidence: `impl/stage-p2c-r1/bin/bishengir-compile` (sha256 `812c5cb1…`, stub phrase 0 / fallback phrase 2) vs `impl/stage-p2b-r1/…` (stub phrase 1); `verify/tests/scripts/run_p2_suite_v4.py` sha256 `4ce88ad7…` / 136,604 B, `t_a13` `:1464-1520`; the v3→v4 diff (`/tmp/v3v4.diff`, 205 lines) showing no `t_a15`/`t_r1` hunk; `verify/logs/suite-v4/20261014T203225Z-1411318/suite-summary.json` sha256 `8fe85678b60cbfdf43b94a5b97f8a4b498dbc0c95429fc356b6fd577e8a0ac1a` (46 rows: 7 PASS / 1 FAIL / 38 NOT_RUN); `lead/r9-review/STEP8-VERDICT-FINAL.txt` `:105`; `docs/status.md` 追加 234 (the R6 adjudication) and 追加 237 (this dispatch). Sibling rules: `unsatisfiable-criterion-is-a-defect-to-prove` (prove it from PRE-run sources; keep the old literal verbatim), `check-literals-must-be-measured-against-the-artifact` (a literal must be exercised against the real artifact). |

就地取代；`lead/r9-review/STEP9-EVIDENCE-MAP.txt`（Lead 自撰工作笔记，**非证据**）的 OPEN QUESTION 一节标注为**已被取代**。 | **Evidence:** `docs/status.md` 追加 256；`R1-LINKED-CONSUMPTION-INVESTIGATION.txt` CORRECTION 3（`:151-272`）；`decisions.md` D112/D113/D120；run-of-record ELF `2c4247c3…`；仪器 `079bf9bb…`。 Family: `a-retraction-must-reach-every-derived-artifact`（D120 命名的 PRE-231 向前传播，本次由 Lead 复现）、`absence-needs-direct-verification`（“环境无此仪器”的负断言未枚举候选）、`instrument-identity-is-not-its-version-banner`（五个同版本候选仅一个解码 AIV）、`run-of-record-verdicts-are-superseded-by-reruns`。 |
| D128 | **P2 Final Gate 裁定：P2 有界接受（SCOPED APPROVE），P3 门禁保持关闭。** 依据 Challenger 正式 verdict 原文（`EV/resume-04/challenger/CHALLENGER-FINAL-VERDICT.txt`，sha256 `18af1997b5181388ddcde1b049b83d6a3d7669fc2ce84abab3394297766c3220` / 12,689 B，由 Lead 从 Challenger transcript 代落盘——Challenger 是只读审查者、不写文件）。四项有界缺陷（V5 类型化载体、V6(c) GM 地址链、函数体保护、V6 同步顺序）全部关闭；修复后身份 `LateInputVerifier.cpp` `e19080ab…`、`bishengir-opt` `9ea6f21b…`、`bishengir-compile` `2206d8c5…`，**未沿用任何旧 SHA**。三个新反例（wrong-attr-type pb32 / C-only offset / V-MTE3-after-store）修复前接受、修复后按正确 V 诊断拒绝。冻结 fixture 的 A5 产物 pre/post-fix **逐字节相同**（`2c4247c3…`/4,448 B，四个 kernel.o 含两次确定性对照），故 **P2 原设备 Binary 无需重建**。矩阵 46 行 = 45 PASS / 1 NOT_RUN（C3），并**明确登记为历史仪器混合体**，不是 46 项新二进制检查。普通 RegBase SIMD 完整 ELF 保留 **BASELINE_BLOCKED**（新旧臂同因：undefined `_mlir_ciface_vadd_1d_float`、同 9,072 B prelink 对象 `7200a267…`），**不谎报 PASS**。 | **裁定 + 一条 record 规则。** 三处记录性更正（`:703-708` 注释高估旧检查、`singleEntryBlock` 多块 `front()`、工作小结中的"468"）一律 **RECORD / DO NOT REPAIR**：改注释须重建，会改变已被封存裁决所判定的二进制身份 `9ea6f21b…`，即**用记录准确性换掉证据同一性**——不值。受限能力（固定 VADD 形态、替代地址表达式、多 pge、动态 UB/ACL/数值）按 goal §4.3 登记而不修，不开通用验证器项目。另：本轮发现并拦截一处**由修复自身引入的回归**（store 的 GM 操作数在 operand 1，固定取 operand 0 会误拒冻结基线），印证 goal §5"若失败由 P2 修改引入，则必须修复，不得豁免"。 |
| D129 | **更正 D128 与 追加 257 §3 的一处**归属**错误：store-GM-operand 回归是 Implementor 首先发现的，不是 Lead。** 我在两处写成"Lead 发现并回报/由 Lead 在验收前拦下"。实测时间线（来源：Implementor transcript 的 tool_use/text 时间戳，非其自述）：**14:30:13Z** 它自己的 runner 看到基线被拒并写下 *"the baseline regressed — it must accept"*；**14:30:23Z** 独立得出根因 *"The store's GM operand is operand 1, not 0. Let me select by address space."*；**14:30:24Z** 落地该修改；而 Lead 的报告文件 ctime 为 **14:29:59Z**、**14:45:28Z** 才送达（含我提出的同一最小修复）。即两件**不同仪器**（我的直接调用 vs 它的 runner）在约 14 秒内各自命中同一缺陷，我**独立复现**了它，但**不是首先发现者**。该回归**未进入任何被接受的二进制**（首建 `27fc3feb…` 已 superseded；证据面为 `9ea6f21b…`）。 | **裁定：RECORD, 就地更正 + 追加，不改已封存字节。** `docs/current-state.md` 为活文档，已就地改写（gen2 快照 `current-state.gen2-pre-correction.md`，`533b0b23…`/14,473 B 保留）；`status.md` 追加 257 与 `decisions.md` D128 属追加式记录，**已封存的字节不动**，其错误句子由本行与追加 258 取代（前缀哈希 `a4d39f90da1d1b65906d5890012de2d7c112af2b12d12288f9a8cce05b696d6c`/1,567,660 B 复验未变，被替换字面量在新区域内唯一）。**机制**：这是一次*credit* 归属错误而非数值错误——它与 D128 同批写入，而 D128 的核心内容（四项缺陷关闭、身份、A5 不变量、BASELINE_BLOCKED、RECORD/DO-NOT-REPAIR）**不受影响**；"谁先发现"既不改变任何判决也不改变任何哈希，但**它会伪造审查独立性**，而审查独立正是本项目全部裁决的依据。**触发原因**：我把自己"独立复现"这件事写成了"发现"——复现者的报告与发现者的报告在同一天、同一缺陷上并存时，两者最容易在记录里合并成一句。**规则**：当一名 teammate 的自述与你自己的测量指向同一事实时，**先在它的 transcript 里定位它的时间戳**再决定动词（discovered / independently reproduced / confirmed）；一个能自证的动词，必须由"谁在何时先说出"来支撑，而不是由"我也测到了"来支撑。 |

## D130 — 就地改写前必须快照：同一轮里对一件工件做了、对另一件没做（2026-10-15）

**裁定**：`P2-FINAL-GATE-DELIVERABLES.txt` 的 rev 1（`3e2b68f77b041e8ae91834d46f4cf0705e0b9a4005b6c16e08d58ed3e7d0a23f`，10,041 B）
字节**已不可恢复**，登记为证据 LOSS，不得改写为"预期漂移已满足"。rev 2 为现行
（`dba9e7e1d70c6a57b191a99593f3c2a26742cd7851ca0071f37cbe1dbf9c6b60`，13,359 B）。

**事实**：D129 归属更正后，该索引有三行过时（gen 记法、旧 sweep 列为现行、一处哈希标签与值不符）。
我更正时**就地改写、未先 `cp -p` 快照**。同一轮里 `docs/current-state.md` 被改两次，
两次都先做了快照（gen 1 `4307866895…`/6,112 B、gen 2 `533b0b23…`/14,473 B，均仍可复算）。

**为什么这是缺陷而不是风格问题**：快照规则不是"本轮记得快照"这一件事，而是**对每一件将被就地改写的
工件**各自成立。对 A 做了、对 B 没做，其后果不是"少一份副本"，而是 B 的旧世代变成一个**没有主体的哈希**：
"rev 1 曾说 X"这句话从此无人可核。`current-state.md` 的两代快照恰恰证明了正确形状是可行的、且成本极低。

**检索范围（使"不存在"有界）**：`os.walk` 遍历 `hivm-poc-evidence/`、`hivm-poc-control/`、`/tmp/claude-1071/`，
按精确大小 10,041 B 取候选（0 个）再逐个 sha256 比对 → 未找到。诚实表述为"该检索未找到"；
不同大小（如重新换行）或三个根之外的副本不在此法覆盖内。

**损失另记**：`EV/resume-04/lead/LOST-GENERATION-deliverables-index-rev1.txt`
（`fdd28e399c8cc78d6cf15d4ae62d29f337f0b4ba24580034556b42d02e23667b`/2,707 B），不依赖于它所涉及的文件。
被改写区域已在 rev 2 内逐字引用。

**绑定方向**：`IDENTITY-SWEEP-FINAL-gen3.txt` 以 sha256 引用 rev 2；rev 2 刻意不打印 sweep 的 sha256——
两件互相指认哈希的工件无法都最后写。绑定单向：sweep → rev 2，sweep 自身身份写在 sweep 内。

**不影响**：无任何 verdict、计数或二进制身份依赖 rev 1 的字节。P2 结论不变。

## D131 — 复核清单不钉哈希，取消索引内自指版本号（2026-10-15）

**裁定**：`IDENTITY-SWEEP-FINAL-gen3.txt` 为现行身份复核清单，**17/17 复现**，其自身 sha256 与 byte size
写在文件内部；`docs/status.md` / `decisions.md` **不再转录该值**。该清单单向绑定 deliverables 索引现行内容
（`c9db8164…`/16,704 B）；索引不打印清单哈希。

**触发事实**：追加 260 与 追加 261 各钉过一次清单哈希（`cd51ae1a…`、`14aef64b…`），两次都在下一次写作时
失效。这不是笔误而是结构性：清单的生成晚于它绑定的工件，记录又晚于清单，链上任何一环写"下一环的哈希"
都会在下一笔失效。索引内同样：先写"rev 2 为现行"、后改"rev 3 为现行"，同样每笔即过时。

**唯一自洽的方向是单向**：清单 → 索引（清单最后写）；记录只指向路径，不替清单记账。
与 `an-oracle-in-an-amendable-file-needs-an-external-anchor` 不冲突：那里要外部锚的是**冻结**判定值，
这里是**每轮重生成**的复核清单；对后者正确形状是工件自带身份。

**另**：deliverables 索引自 rev 3 起，每次就地改写前先 `cp -p` 快照（`.rev3.snapshot.txt` `2cb626f0…`、
`.rev4.snapshot.txt` `cb8a1060…`），落实 D130 的教训。rev 1/2/3-pre 三代字节已丢失并登记。

**追加 260/261 字节不改**，其引用的两个哈希限定为历史值。结论不变。

## D132 — 更正 D131：文件不能包含自己的哈希；清单身份由**锚文件**承载（2026-10-15）

**裁定**：D131 / 追加 262 中"清单自身 sha256 与 byte size 写在文件内部"这一表述**不可实现**（文件无法包含
自己的 sha256），予以更正。可行形状为：**锚文件**承载清单的 sha256，二者由**同一个脚本一步生成**，
脚本在全部行复现之前**拒绝写出任何东西**。D131 的其余部分（取消记录钉清单哈希、绑定单向、索引不自称版本号）
**继续成立**，并被本条加强。

**落地**：
- 锚 `EV/resume-04/lead/IDENTITY-SWEEP-FINAL-gen3.sha256.txt`；清单 `…/IDENTITY-SWEEP-FINAL-gen3.txt`（不含自身哈希）。
- 生成器 `EV/resume-04/lead/regenerate-identity-sweep.py`：先校验全部行（当前 23/23），全部复现才写出；
  覆盖前把上一代清单与锚 `cp -p` 旁置（`…superseded-<旧sha前8>.txt`），使被取代字节仍可复算。
- 实测拒绝路径：用过期 claim 运行 → rc=1、`REFUSING TO WRITE: 19/21`、**无半截输出**。
- 实测可重复：连跑两次，清单与锚哈希不变。

**有意排除**：`docs/status.md` / `docs/decisions.md` 不入清单——它们是每阶段追加的活记录，
钉其哈希会在下一阶段立即失效；其完整性由每次追加的前缀哈希复核主张（更弱、但不会自毁）。

**性质**：记录性更正；不改 verdict、计数或二进制身份。P2 结论不变。

## D133 — 生成器缺陷：旁置命名、幂等双胞胎、普查自指回环（2026-10-15）

**裁定**：`regenerate-identity-sweep.py` 的三处缺陷按"先测量、再修"处理，全部为**结构性**修复：

1. **旁置命名**：后缀曾追加到整个文件名（产出 `…sha256.txt.superseded-XXXX.txt`）。改为在最后扩展名前插入，
   统一 `<kind>.superseded-<old-sha8>.txt`，收进 `superseded-sweep-generations/`。
2. **幂等双胞胎**：幂等重跑会把活文件按自身 sha 旁置，制造逐字节相同的"双胞胎"。加"字节相同则不旁置"判据；
   实测连续三次运行文件集与哈希不变。
3. **普查自指回环**：普查副本曾被旁置进**普查自己所枚举的目录**，既破坏幂等性、又被误分类为 `sweep` 世代。
   修复：**派生文件不做旁置**（枚举某目录的工件不得被旁置进该目录）；`superseded_name()` 对无法归类的输入
   **抛错**而非默认 `sweep`——默认值正是误分类来源。

**另**：普查改由生成器**同一步**写出（此前独立生成，实测已脱节一次：写 9 而目录 11）。

**普查口径**：`SUPERSEDED-SWEEP-CENSUS.txt` 统计的是**盘上文件**，不是"历史上共有多少代"；
它不是单调量（幂等重跑不变；一次合并因去重而**减少**）。早于 23:19 的世代从未旁置、**刻意不列入**普查——
列一行就等于声称存在一个文件。

**性质**：工具性/记录性修复，不改任何 verdict、计数或二进制身份。

## D134 — 未快照的就地改写：可恢复性取决于"是否逐字记录"，而非"是否快照"（2026-10-15）

**事实**：收尾索引 `P2-FINAL-GATE-DELIVERABLES.txt` 在本轮被就地改写一次而**未先快照**——即追加 260/261 / D130
记录的同一缺陷在同一文件上重演。改写内容：撤回索引里残留的"清单身份写在清单内部"（D132 已判定其不可能），
并修正两处过期行数（16/16、current-state gen 5）。

**裁定**：这一代**已逐字节恢复**。改写 #1 的三处替换在记录中逐字存在；将之重新施加于改写前的快照
`P2-FINAL-GATE-DELIVERABLES.rev10.snapshot.txt`（`6db60dae…`/20,030 B），得到
`1b16d7d161496d07f82a318f7205928e816cbe11ab6d94a72e8e0c7e4b8af33a`/20,584 B，与当时实测值
sha256 与 byte size 均相同；存为 `…rev11-recovered.snapshot.txt`。

**规则（收紧 D130 §(a)）**：**可恢复性不取决于是否快照，而取决于改写是否被逐字记录。**
- rev 1/2/3-pre：无快照 **且** 替换内容未逐字记录 → **丢失**，不可重建。
- rev 11（本条）：无快照 **但** 替换内容逐字记录 → **已恢复**，逐字节相符。

因此"D130 说未快照即丢失"这一表述**过强**：正确的判据是两个条件同时不成立才叫丢失。
本裁定**不改变** rev 1/2/3-pre 仍属丢失的结论。

**纪律**：对活工件就地改写，**先 `cp -p` 快照**，**并且把替换内容逐字写进记录**——两者都做，才在
"没快照"或"没记录"任一失误下都还留一条退路。不改任何 verdict、计数或二进制身份。

## D135 — 复核清单的行数曾把一次测量算成两次（2026-10-15）

**事实**：复核清单枚举 deliverables 索引快照时把 `rev7`、`rev8` 各列一行，实测两者**逐字节相同**
（两次 `cp -p` 之间无编辑），即**同一世代的两次快照**，不是两个世代。清单按行报 `28/28`，
把**一次测量计成两次**。与 D083/D084 同类，本轮第二次犯。

**裁定**：移除重复行；生成器加入**孪生守卫**——被枚举两行哈希相同即**拒绝写出**
（已实测可触发：插入重复行 → rc=1，打印冲突两行）。清单自报数改为"行数 + 不同哈希数"且**同属一个全集**
（第一版只把索引行算进行数、未算进不同哈希数，打印 `31/31 … 30 DISTINCT`，读起来像仍有孪生）。
现为 `31/31`、`31 DISTINCT`。**不改任何 verdict 或二进制身份。**

**规则**：枚举型证据的"N/N"必须在**同一全集**上给出"行数"与"不同主体数"两个数；两者不等即说明有重复，
重复必须移除或显式标注，而不是靠读者自行折算。守卫必须**实测可触发**——不可触发的守卫读起来像覆盖。

## D136 — 部分撤回比不撤回更危险：同一步产出的工件，身份同生共死（2026-10-15）

**事实**：追加 267 转录了复核清单的哈希（违反 D131/D132），追加 268 撤回之，但 268 同时写"锚与普查的哈希
不作废"。**该边界划错了**：锚、普查与清单由生成器**同一步**产出，清单重生成时三者一起重生成，
故三个值**同生共死**；267 转录的四个哈希（清单/锚/普查/索引）**全部**过时（索引只是因为期间未再编辑而侥幸相符）。
追加 269 更正。

**规则**：撤回一个值时必须先问**"这个值是谁在何时产出的"**——同一步/同一命令产出的多个值构成一个**原子身份组**，
要么全撤要么全不撤。只撤其中一个会留下其余过时值被读者当作仍然有效，**比完全不撤更危险**，因为它读起来像已查清。
推论：记录不应转录任何活工件的哈希（D131）；若已转录，撤回时按**产出步骤**划边界，不按"哪几个看起来像会变"划。

## D137 — 枚举型证据的每一行必须自带可复跑的全部信息（2026-10-15）

**事实**：复核清单表头一直声明"绝对路径已打印，故可按原文复跑"，而**行内只有标签+sha256+字节数**——
该声明从未成立（修复前 37 行全部如此）。这与 D042/B12a 同类：**表头里的字面量是一条断言，必须被行使**。

**裁定**：行格式改为携带**绝对路径**；并新增**读者视角**独立复核（解析文件、逐行重算、不借助生成器）：
`37/37` 复现、`37` 个不同哈希、无孪生。

**规则**：
1. 枚举型证据（清单、普查、矩阵）的**每一行**必须自带重算所需的全部信息（路径 + 期望值 + 期望大小），
   否则"可复跑"只是表头的一句愿望。
2. **解析式复核必须报告未匹配项**，不得静默跳过：我的第一版复核脚本正则漏掉两行、报 33 而非 35，
   因为我把未解析行当成不存在。**枚举胜过计数**；报告未匹配项是枚举的前提。
